{"parentUuid":"691ccc67-a2ec-4b56-99e7-233da595627b","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"2a5fa244-5ee5-45be-a093-94e0e37d9a8d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016q21koLZFvGmdcHhUy9oP2","type":"tool_result","content":"# MP2 Setup Exercise — Context-Threshold Warning Hook\n\n**Course:** IPHS 400 Frontiers of AI, Kenyon College, Fall 2026 (Jon Chun)\n**Where this goes:** Student manual, Part 1 (setup), right after the template repo is cloned and `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` has run.\n**Time:** 20–30 minutes. **Token cost:** under 3% of a 5-hour window (the scripts cost nothing at runtime; hooks run outside the model).\n**Rubric link:** Factor B, item B6 \"session hygiene\" (3 pts). The judge checks that the hook works and reads `docs/process/compaction-log.md`.\n**Verified against:** Claude Code hooks and status-line docs as of 2026-09-22. Record your own version with `claude --version` in `notes/setup_check.md`.\n\n---\n\n## Why you are building this\n\nPocock's loop runs one ticket per fresh context: `/clear`, then `/implement #N`. Most tickets finish well inside one window. A long ticket does not, and a model working in a very full context forgets the ticket's acceptance criteria and starts to drift from the spec. The course rule is:\n\n- **Short ticket:** `/clear` before it. Nothing else.\n- **Long ticket:** once context passes **50–60%**, `/compact` at the next **green test** (the end of a `/tdd` red-green-refactor cycle), never in the middle of a failing test, and always with a focus note.\n\nYou cannot follow that rule if you cannot see the number. This exercise gives you a live meter and a warning that fires at the right moment.\n\n## How it works\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    CC[\"Claude Code session\"] -- \"session JSON on stdin<br/>(every refresh)\" --> SL[\"ctx_statusline.py<br/>prints: [Opus] ctx 47% ▓▓▓▓▓░░░░░\"]\n    SL -- \"writes\" --> ST[\".claude/state/ctx_&lt;session&gt;.json<br/>{pct: 47.2}\"]\n    CC -- \"Stop event<br/>(end of every Claude turn)\" --> G[\"ctx_guard.py\"]\n    ST -- \"reads\" --> G\n    G -- \"first time over 50 / 60 / 80%\" --> W[\"⚠ systemMessage shown to YOU\"]\n    CC -- \"PreCompact event\" --> L[\"ctx_compact_log.py\"]\n    L -- \"appends\" --> LOG[\"docs/process/compaction-log.md<br/>(grading evidence)\"]\n```\n\nThree facts from the Claude Code docs make this design work:\n\n1. **The status line already receives the context percentage** as `context_window.used_percentage` on stdin. The status line is the customizable bar under your prompt.\n2. **A `Stop` hook fires every time Claude finishes a turn.** The end of a turn is where a natural boundary (a green test) happens, so that is when the warning should appear.\n3. **A hook's `systemMessage` is shown to the user, not to Claude.** The warning talks to you, and does not pollute Claude's context.\n\nA hook cannot type `/compact` for you. Slash commands are yours. The hook only warns; you decide.\n\nEach herdr pane or terminal tab running Claude has its own `session_id`, so parallel sessions keep separate meters and never trip each other's warnings.\n\n## The four files\n\n| File | Job | Runs when |\n|---|---|---|\n| `.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py` | Shows the meter; records the percentage | Every status-line refresh |\n| `.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py` | Warns at 50 / 60 / 80% | End of every Claude turn (`Stop`) |\n| `.claude/hooks/ctx_compact_log.py` | Logs each compaction and its focus note | Before every compaction (`PreCompact`) |\n| `tests/test_ctx_guard.py` | Proves the logic works | When `/tdd` or `/code-review` runs the suite |\n\n---\n\n## Step 1 — Check Python\n\nIn Ghostty (macOS) or Windows Terminal (WSL2), inside your `iphs400-mp2-cms` folder:\n\n```bash\npython3 --version\n```\n\n**Expected:** `Python 3.9` or newer (the `uv` setup from the setup manual gives you 3.12).\n**If you see `command not found`:** macOS runs `xcode-select --install`; WSL2 runs `sudo apt install -y python3`. Then retry.\n\n## Step 2 — Open the hook files\n\nThe template already contains all four files. Open the folder in VS Code:\n\n```bash\ncode .\n```\n\nIn the Explorer panel, expand `.claude/hooks/`. You should see `ctx_statusline.py`, `ctx_guard.py`, and `ctx_compact_log.py`.\n\n**If `.claude` is not visible:** VS Code hides nothing by default, but macOS Finder hides dot-folders. Use VS Code, or run `ls -la .claude/hooks` in the terminal.\n\n## Step 3 — Read the status-line script\n\nThis file is complete. Read it so you know what it does; you will explain it in your report.\n\n```python\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Status line for IPHS 400 MP2.\n\nClaude Code runs this script on every status-line refresh and pipes session\nJSON to stdin. The script does two things:\n  1. prints one line under the prompt:  [Opus] ctx 47% ▓▓▓▓▓░░░░░\n  2. records the context percentage in .claude/state/ctx_<session>.json so the\n     Stop hook (ctx_guard.py) can read it at the end of each turn.\nIt must never crash: a broken status line hides the meter, nothing else.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef project_dir(data: dict) -> Path:\n    ws = data.get(\"workspace\") or {}\n    return Path(\n        os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\")\n        or ws.get(\"project_dir\")\n        or ws.get(\"current_dir\")\n        or os.getcwd()\n    )\n\n\ndef bar(pct: float, width: int = 10) -> str:\n    filled = max(0, min(width, round(pct / 100 * width)))\n    return \"▓\" * filled + \"░\" * (width - filled)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    try:\n        data = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except Exception:\n        print(\"ctx ?\")\n        return\n\n    model = (data.get(\"model\") or {}).get(\"display_name\", \"Claude\")\n    pct = (data.get(\"context_window\") or {}).get(\"used_percentage\")\n    if pct is None:\n        print(f\"[{model}] ctx --\")\n        return\n    pct = float(pct)\n\n    session = data.get(\"session_id\") or \"default\"\n    state_dir = project_dir(data) / \".claude\" / \"state\"\n    try:\n        state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        tmp = state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}.json.tmp\"\n        tmp.write_text(json.dumps({\"pct\": pct, \"ts\": time.time()}))\n        tmp.replace(state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}.json\")  # atomic swap\n    except OSError:\n        pass  # meter still prints even if the disk write fails\n\n    flag = \"  ⚠ compact at next green test\" if pct >= 60 else \"\"\n    print(f\"[{model}] ctx {pct:.0f}% {bar(pct)}{flag}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n```\n\n## Step 4 — Write the Stop hook's logic (the exercise)\n\nThe template ships `ctx_guard.py` with everything written **except the body of `decide()`**, which raises `NotImplementedError`. Seven of the ten tests in `tests/test_ctx_guard.py` fail until you finish it. This is your first practice run of the loop on something tiny.\n\nIn a fresh Claude Code session:\n\n```text\n/clear\n/tdd Implement decide() in .claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py so the failing tests in tests/test_ctx_guard.py pass. Do not change the tests or any other function.\n```\n\n`/tdd` runs the tests, watches them fail, writes the code, and runs them again until they pass. You do not type `pytest` yourself.\n\nThe rules `decide()` must follow:\n\n- Map the percentage to a band: 0 = below 50, 1 = 50+, 2 = 60+, 3 = 80+ (the thresholds come from `thresholds()`).\n- **Warn once per upward crossing.** Going from 52% to 55% must stay quiet.\n- **Re-arm after compaction.** When usage drops (after `/compact` or `/clear`), remember the lower band so the next crossing warns again.\n\nWhen the tests pass, compare your version with the complete file below.\n\n\n## Step 5 — Read the compaction logger\n\nThis file is complete. It writes the evidence the judge reads for rubric item B6.\n\n```python\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"PreCompact hook: log every compaction to docs/process/compaction-log.md.\n\nThis is grading evidence for rubric item B6 (session hygiene):\n  - trigger \"manual\" + a focus note  = you compacted on purpose (good)\n  - trigger \"auto\"                   = the session overflowed (fix the habit)\nNever exits 2: exit 2 on PreCompact would BLOCK compaction.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nHEADER = (\n    \"# Compaction log\\n\\n\"\n    \"| UTC time | Session | Trigger | Focus note |\\n\"\n    \"|---|---|---|---|\\n\"\n)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    try:\n        event = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except Exception:\n        return\n    project = Path(os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\") or event.get(\"cwd\") or os.getcwd())\n    log = project / \"docs\" / \"process\" / \"compaction-log.md\"\n    log.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    if not log.exists():\n        log.write_text(HEADER)\n    note = (event.get(\"custom_instructions\") or \"\").replace(\"|\", \"/\").replace(\"\\n\", \" \").strip()\n    row = \"| {} | {} | {} | {} |\\n\".format(\n        datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\"),\n        (event.get(\"session_id\") or \"?\")[:8],\n        event.get(\"trigger\", \"unknown\"),\n        note or \"(none)\",\n    )\n    with log.open(\"a\") as f:\n        f.write(row)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    try:\n        main()\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    sys.exit(0)\n```\n\n**Warning:** never change its exit code to 2. On `PreCompact`, exit code 2 **blocks compaction**, which would lock you inside an overflowing session.\n\n## Step 6 — Confirm the hooks are registered\n\nThe template already registers all three in `.claude/settings.json`, together with\nthe `SessionEnd` transcript saver. Read that file now so you know what is wired up:\n\n```bash\ncat .claude/settings.json\n```\n\n**Expected:** a `statusLine` entry plus `Stop`, `PreCompact`, and `SessionEnd`\nhooks, each pointing at a script in `.claude/hooks/`. Check it is valid JSON:\n\n```bash\npython3 -m json.tool .claude/settings.json > /dev/null && echo \"settings OK\"\n```\n\n**Expected:** `settings OK`. If you ever edit this file and see\n`Expecting ',' delimiter`, a comma is missing at the line number shown.\n\n## Step 7 — Keep the state files out of git\n\nThe meter's scratch files are per-session and per-laptop. They must not be committed:\n\n```bash\ngrep -qxF '.claude/state/' .gitignore || echo '.claude/state/' >> .gitignore\ntail -3 .gitignore\n```\n\n**Expected:** `.claude/state/` appears in the last lines. (`docs/process/compaction-log.md` *is* committed; it is evidence.)\n\n## Step 8 — Test the scripts without Claude\n\nPipe fake session data in, exactly as Claude Code would:\n\n```bash\nexport CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PWD\"\n\necho '{\"session_id\":\"demo\",\"model\":{\"display_name\":\"Opus\"},\"context_window\":{\"used_percentage\":63}}' \\\n  | python3 .claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\n\necho '{\"session_id\":\"demo\",\"hook_event_name\":\"Stop\"}' | python3 .claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\necho '{\"session_id\":\"demo\",\"hook_event_name\":\"Stop\"}' | python3 .claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\n\nrm -f .claude/state/ctx_demo*.json\n```\n\n**Expected output, in order:**\n\n1. `[Opus] ctx 63% ▓▓▓▓▓▓░░░░  ⚠ compact at next green test`\n2. `{\"systemMessage\": \"Context 63%: at the next green test run  /compact keep: ticket #N acceptance criteria, what is left, files touched\"}`\n3. *(nothing)*: the second call is silent because it already warned once.\n\n## Step 9 — Test it live with low thresholds\n\nStart Claude with thresholds so low that the first reply trips them:\n\n```bash\nCTX_WARN_PCT=1 CTX_COMPACT_PCT=2 claude\n```\n\n1. Look under the prompt. **Expected:** `[<model>] ctx N% ▓░░░░░░░░░`.\n2. Type `/hooks`. **Expected:** `Stop` and `PreCompact` each show 1+ hooks with the source `Project Settings`. Press Esc.\n3. Type `say hi`. After Claude answers, **expected:** a warning line starting `Context N%:`.\n4. Type `/compact keep: hook test`. Then open `docs/process/compaction-log.md`. **Expected:** a new row ending `| manual | keep: hook test |`.\n5. Type `/exit`. From now on start Claude normally (`claude`), and the real 50/60/80% thresholds apply.\n\nDelete the test row from `docs/process/compaction-log.md` so your log starts clean.\n\n## Step 10 — Commit\n\n```text\nCommit the context-threshold hooks, settings.json, .gitignore, and tests/test_ctx_guard.py with the message \"setup: context-threshold warning hook\" and push.\n```\n\nCheck on GitHub that the commit appears, and that `.claude/state/` is **not** in the repo.\n\n---\n\n## Using it during tickets\n\n| You see | Do this |\n|---|---|\n| Meter under 50% | Keep working. |\n| `Context 5x%: … plan a /compact` | Let the current red-green cycle finish. |\n| `Context 6x%: at the next green test run /compact keep: …` | As soon as the tests are green, type `/compact keep: ticket #N acceptance criteria, what is left, files touched` (with the real ticket number). |\n| `Context 8x%: compact now …` | Compact immediately, or `/handoff` and start a new session. Afterward, ask whether the ticket should be split with `/to-tickets`. |\n| A log row with trigger `auto` | The session overflowed and Claude compacted on its own. It is not an error, but the judge counts it against B6; next time, compact yourself at 60%. |\n| Starting the next short ticket | `/clear`. No compaction needed. |\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n| Symptom | Likely cause | Fix |\n|---|---|---|\n| No meter under the prompt | `statusLine` block missing, or JSON invalid | Rerun the Step 6 JSON check; restart Claude |\n| Meter shows `ctx --` | Your Claude Code version does not send `context_window` | Run `claude update`, restart |\n| Meter works, never any warning | Stop hook not registered, or the path is wrong | `/hooks` should list it; if it shows `Failed with non-blocking status code`, the path in `settings.json` is wrong |\n| `python3: command not found` inside the hook | Hook shell has a different PATH | Replace `python3` in `settings.json` with the full path from `which python3` |\n| Compaction log never appears | `PreCompact` entry missing | Recheck Step 6 with `/hooks` |\n| Focus note column says `(none)` | You ran `/compact` with no note, or your version names the field differently | Always type a note; if it still shows `(none)`, tell the instructor your `claude --version` |\n| Warnings pop up in the wrong pane | Should not happen: each session has its own state file | Delete `.claude/state/` and restart both sessions |\n\nIf none of these fix it, follow the self-help ladder in the manual: reread the error, run `/hooks`, ask Claude to explain the error before changing anything, check the [hooks reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks) and [status line docs](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/statusline), log it with `/teach-log`, then email the instructor with the error pasted in.\n\n## Saved? ✓\n\nBefore moving on, all of these must be true:\n\n- [ ] `.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py`, `ctx_guard.py`, `ctx_compact_log.py` committed\n- [ ] `.claude/settings.json` contains `statusLine`, `Stop`, and `PreCompact` entries, and passes the JSON check\n- [ ] `tests/test_ctx_guard.py` committed, and `/tdd` reported all 10 tests passing\n- [ ] `.claude/state/` listed in `.gitignore` and absent from GitHub\n- [ ] `claude --version` recorded in `notes/setup_check.md`\n\n## Complete test file (template contents)\n\n```python\n\"\"\"Tests for the context-threshold hooks (IPHS 400 MP2, setup exercise).\"\"\"\nimport importlib.util\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nHOOKS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / \".claude\" / \"hooks\"\n\n\ndef load(name):\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, HOOKS / f\"{name}.py\")\n    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)\n    return mod\n\n\nguard = load(\"ctx_guard\")\n\n\ndef run(script, payload, project, **env):\n    return subprocess.run(\n        [sys.executable, str(HOOKS / script)],\n        input=json.dumps(payload),\n        capture_output=True,\n        text=True,\n        env={**os.environ, \"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\": str(project), **env},\n    )\n\n\n# ---- pure logic -----------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_silent_below_50():\n    assert guard.decide(49.9, guard.NONE) == (None, guard.NONE)\n\n\ndef test_warns_once_at_50():\n    msg, band = guard.decide(52, guard.NONE)\n    assert band == guard.WARN and \"52%\" in msg\n    assert guard.decide(55, band) == (None, guard.WARN)  # no nagging\n\n\ndef test_compact_message_at_60_names_focus_note():\n    msg, band = guard.decide(61, guard.WARN)\n    assert band == guard.COMPACT and \"/compact keep:\" in msg\n\n\ndef test_urgent_at_80_suggests_split():\n    msg, band = guard.decide(81, guard.COMPACT)\n    assert band == guard.URGENT and \"/to-tickets\" in msg\n\n\ndef test_rearms_after_compaction():\n    _, band = guard.decide(20, guard.COMPACT)   # usage dropped after /compact\n    assert band == guard.NONE\n    msg, _ = guard.decide(51, band)             # next crossing warns again\n    assert msg is not None\n\n\ndef test_thresholds_from_env(monkeypatch):\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CTX_WARN_PCT\", \"1\")\n    msg, _ = guard.decide(2, guard.NONE)\n    assert msg is not None\n\n\n# ---- end to end: status line -> Stop hook ---------------------------------\n\ndef test_statusline_then_stop_hook(tmp_path):\n    sl = run(\"ctx_statusline.py\",\n             {\"session_id\": \"s1\", \"model\": {\"display_name\": \"Opus\"},\n              \"context_window\": {\"used_percentage\": 63.4}}, tmp_path)\n    assert \"ctx 63%\" in sl.stdout\n    out = run(\"ctx_guard.py\", {\"session_id\": \"s1\", \"hook_event_name\": \"Stop\"}, tmp_path)\n    assert out.returncode == 0\n    assert \"/compact\" in json.loads(out.stdout)[\"systemMessage\"]\n    again = run(\"ctx_guard.py\", {\"session_id\": \"s1\", \"hook_event_name\": \"Stop\"}, tmp_path)\n    assert again.stdout.strip() == \"\"           # warned once, then quiet\n\n\ndef test_stop_hook_silent_without_data(tmp_path):\n    out = run(\"ctx_guard.py\", {\"session_id\": \"nobody\"}, tmp_path)\n    assert out.returncode == 0 and out.stdout == \"\"\n\n\ndef test_stop_hook_survives_garbage_stdin(tmp_path):\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(HOOKS / \"ctx_guard.py\")],\n                         input=\"not json\", capture_output=True, text=True,\n                         env={**os.environ, \"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\": str(tmp_path)})\n    assert out.returncode == 0\n\n\ndef test_precompact_logs_focus_note(tmp_path):\n    run(\"ctx_compact_log.py\",\n        {\"session_id\": \"abcdef123\", \"trigger\": \"manual\",\n         \"custom_instructions\": \"keep: ticket #5 criteria\"}, tmp_path)\n    log = (tmp_path / \"docs/process/compaction-log.md\").read_text()\n    assert \"| manual | keep: ticket #5 criteria |\" in log\n```","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"6f07279e-a83d-4980-bfae-7aa8822215e6","timestamp":"2026-10-13T00:13:11.281Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"# MP2 Setup Exercise — The script does two things:\n  1. prints one line under the prompt:  [Opus] ctx 47% ▓▓▓▓▓░░░░░\n  2. records the context percentage in .claude/state/ctx_<session>.json so the\n     Stop hook (ctx_guard.py) can read it at the end of each turn.\nIt must never crash: a broken status line hides the meter, nothing else.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef project_dir(data: dict) -> Path:\n    ws = data.get(\"workspace\") or {}\n    return Path(\n        os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\")\n        or ws.get(\"project_dir\")\n        or ws.get(\"current_dir\")\n        or os.getcwd()\n    )\n\n\ndef bar(pct: float, width: int = 10) -> str:\n    filled = max(0, min(width, round(pct / 100 * width)))\n    return \"▓\" * filled + \"░\" * (width - filled)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    try:\n        data = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except Exception:\n        print(\"ctx ?\")\n        return\n\n    model = (data.get(\"model\") or {}).get(\"display_name\", \"Claude\")\n    pct = (data.get(\"context_window\") or {}).get(\"used_percentage\")\n    if pct is None:\n        print(f\"[{model}] ctx --\")\n        return\n    pct = float(pct)\n\n    session = data.get(\"session_id\") or \"default\"\n    state_dir = project_dir(data) / \".claude\" / \"state\"\n    try:\n        state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        tmp = state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}.json.tmp\"\n        tmp.write_text(json.dumps({\"pct\": pct, \"ts\": time.time()}))\n        tmp.replace(state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}.json\")  # atomic swap\n    except OSError:\n        pass  # meter still prints even if the disk write fails\n\n    flag = \"  ⚠ compact at next green test\" if pct >= 60 else \"\"\n    print(f\"[{model}] ctx {pct:.0f}% {bar(pct)}{flag}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n```\n\n## Step 4 — Write the Stop hook's logic (the exercise)\n\nThe template ships `ctx_guard.py` with everything written **except the body of `decide()`**, which raises `NotImplementedError`. Seven of the ten tests in `tests/test_ctx_guard.py` fail until you finish it. This is your first practice run of the loop on something tiny.\n\nIn a fresh Claude Code session:\n\n```text\n/clear\n/tdd Implement decide() in .claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py so the failing tests in tests/test_ctx_guard.py pass. Do not change the tests or any other function.\n```\n\n`/tdd` runs the tests, watches them fail, writes the code, and runs them again until they pass. You do not type `pytest` yourself.\n\nThe rules `decide()` must follow:\n\n- Map the percentage to a band: 0 = below 50, 1 = 50+, 2 = 60+, 3 = 80+ (the thresholds come from `thresholds()`).\n- **Warn once per upward crossing.** Going from 52% to 55% must stay quiet.\n- **Re-arm after compaction.** When usage drops (after `/compact` or `/clear`), remember the lower band so the next crossing warns again.\n\nWhen the tests pass, compare your version with the complete file below.\n\n\n## Step 5 — Read the compaction logger\n\nThis file is complete. It writes the evidence the judge reads for rubric item B6.\n\n```python\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"PreCompact hook: log every compaction to docs/process/compaction-log.md.\n\nThis is grading evidence for rubric item B6 (session hygiene):\n  - trigger \"manual\" + a focus note  = you compacted on purpose (good)\n  - trigger \"auto\"                   = the session overflowed (fix the habit)\nNever exits 2: exit 2 on PreCompact would BLOCK compaction.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nHEADER = (\n    \"# Compaction log\\n\\n\"\n    \"| UTC time | Session | Trigger | Focus note |\\n\"\n    \"|---|---|---|---|\\n\"\n)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    try:\n        event = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except Exception:\n        return\n    project = Path(os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\") or event.get(\"cwd\") or os.getcwd())\n    log = project / \"docs\" / \"process\" / \"compaction-log.md\"\n    log.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    if not log.exists():\n        log.write_text(HEADER)\n    note = (event.get(\"custom_instructions\") or \"\").replace(\"|\", \"/\").replace(\"\\n\", \" \").strip()\n    row = \"| {} | {} | {} | {} |\\n\".format(\n        datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\"),\n        (event.get(\"session_id\") or \"?\")[:8],\n        event.get(\"trigger\", \"unknown\"),\n        note or \"(none)\",\n    )\n    with log.open(\"a\") as f:\n        f.write(row)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    try:\n        main()\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    sys.exit(0)\n```\n\n**Warning:** never change its exit code to 2. On `PreCompact`, exit code 2 **blocks compaction**, which would lock you inside an overflowing session.\n\n## Step 6 — Confirm the hooks are registered\n\nThe template already registers all three in `.claude/settings.json`, together with\nthe `SessionEnd` transcript saver. Read that file now so you know what is wired up:\n\n```bash\ncat .claude/settings.json\n```\n\n**Expected:** a `statusLine` entry plus `Stop`, `PreCompact`, and `SessionEnd`\nhooks, each pointing at a script in `.claude/hooks/`. Check it is valid JSON:\n\n```bash\npython3 -m json.tool .claude/settings.json > /dev/null && echo \"settings OK\"\n```\n\n**Expected:** `settings OK`. If you ever edit this file and see\n`Expecting ',' delimiter`, a comma is missing at the line number shown.\n\n## Step 7 — Keep the state files out of git\n\nThe meter's scratch files are per-session and per-laptop. They must not be committed:\n\n```bash\ngrep -qxF '.claude/state/' .gitignore || echo '.claude/state/' >> .gitignore\ntail -3 .gitignore\n```\n\n**Expected:** `.claude/state/` appears in the last lines. (`docs/process/compaction-log.md` *is* committed; it is evidence.)\n\n## Step 8 — Test the scripts without Claude\n\nPipe fake session data in, exactly as Claude Code would:\n\n```bash\nexport CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PWD\"\n\necho '{\"session_id\":\"demo\",\"model\":{\"display_name\":\"Opus\"},\"context_window\":{\"used_percentage\":63}}' \\\n  | python3 .claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\n\necho '{\"session_id\":\"demo\",\"hook_event_name\":\"Stop\"}' | python3 .claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\necho '{\"session_id\":\"demo\",\"hook_event_name\":\"Stop\"}' | python3 .claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\n\nrm -f .claude/state/ctx_demo*.json\n```\n\n**Expected output, in order:**\n\n1. `[Opus] ctx 63% ▓▓▓▓▓▓░░░░  ⚠ compact at next green test`\n2. `{\"systemMessage\": \"Context 63%: at the next green test run  /compact keep: ticket #N acceptance criteria, what is left, files touched\"}`\n3. *(nothing)*: the second call is silent because it already warned once.\n\n## Step 9 — Test it live with low thresholds\n\nStart Claude with thresholds so low that the first reply trips them:\n\n```bash\nCTX_WARN_PCT=1 CTX_COMPACT_PCT=2 claude\n```\n\n1. Look under the prompt. **Expected:** `[<model>] ctx N% ▓░░░░░░░░░`.\n2. Type `/hooks`. **Expected:** `Stop` and `PreCompact` each show 1+ hooks with the source `Project Settings`. Press Esc.\n3. Type `say hi`. After Claude answers, **expected:** a warning line starting `Context N%:`.\n4. Type `/compact keep: hook test`. Then open `docs/process/compaction-log.md`. **Expected:** a new row ending `| manual | keep: hook test |`.\n5. Type `/exit`. From now on start Claude normally (`claude`), and the real 50/60/80% thresholds apply.\n\nDelete the test row from `docs/process/compaction-log.md` so your log starts clean.\n\n## Step 10 — Commit\n\n```text\nCommit the context-threshold hooks, settings.json, .gitignore, and tests/test_ctx_guard.py with the message \"setup: context-threshold warning hook\" and push.\n```\n\nCheck on GitHub that the commit appears, and that `.claude/state/` is **not** in the repo.\n\n---\n\n## Using it during tickets\n\n| You see | Do this |\n|---|---|\n| Meter under 50% | Keep working. |\n| `Context 5x%: … plan a /compact` | Let the current red-green cycle finish. |\n| `Context 6x%: at the next green test run /compact keep: …` | As soon as the tests are green, type `/compact keep: ticket #N acceptance criteria, what is left, files touched` (with the real ticket number). |\n| `Context 8x%: compact now …` | Compact immediately, or `/handoff` and start a new session. Afterward, ask whether the ticket should be split with `/to-tickets`. |\n| A log row with trigger `auto` | The session overflowed and Claude compacted on its own. It is not an error, but the judge counts it against B6; next time, compact yourself at 60%. |\n| Starting the next short ticket | `/clear`. No compaction needed. |\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n| Symptom | Likely cause | Fix |\n|---|---|---|\n| No meter under the prompt | `statusLine` block missing, or JSON invalid | Rerun the Step 6 JSON check; restart Claude |\n| Meter shows `ctx --` | Your Claude Code version does not send `context_window` | Run `claude update`, restart |\n| Meter works, never any warning | Stop hook not registered, or the path is wrong | `/hooks` should list it; if it shows `Failed with non-blocking status code`, the path in `settings.json` is wrong |\n| `python3: command not found` inside the hook | Hook shell has a different PATH | Replace `python3` in `settings.json` with the full path from `which python3` |\n| Compaction log never appears | `PreCompact` entry missing | Recheck Step 6 with `/hooks` |\n| Focus note column says `(none)` | You ran `/compact` with no note, or your version names the field differently | Always type a note; if it still shows `(none)`, tell the instructor your `claude --version` |\n| Warnings pop up in the wrong pane | Should not happen: each session has its own state file | Delete `.claude/state/` and restart both sessions |\n\nIf none of these fix it, follow the self-help ladder in the manual: reread the error, run `/hooks`, ask Claude to explain the error before changing anything, check the [hooks reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks) and [status line docs](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/statusline), log it with `/teach-log`, then email the instructor with the error pasted in.\n\n## Saved? ✓\n\nBefore moving on, all of these must be true:\n\n- [ ] `.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py`, `ctx_guard.py`, `ctx_compact_log.py` committed\n- [ ] `.claude/settings.json` contains `statusLine`, `Stop`, and `PreCompact` entries, and passes the JSON check\n- [ ] `tests/test_ctx_guard.py` committed, and `/tdd` reported all 10 tests passing\n- [ ] `.claude/state/` listed in `.gitignore` and absent from GitHub\n- [ ] `claude --version` recorded in `notes/setup_check.md`\n\n## Complete test file (template contents)\n\n```python\n\"\"\"Tests for the context-threshold hooks (IPHS 400 MP2, setup exercise).\"\"\"\nimport importlib.util\nimport It reads the percentage the status line recorded, and\nprints {\"systemMessage\": \"...\"} the first time usage crosses a threshold.\nThe message is shown to YOU, not to Claude. The hook never blocks and never\nruns /compact itself: slash commands are yours to type.\n\nThresholds (override when launching:  CTX_WARN_PCT=1 claude  to test):\n  CTX_WARN_PCT     default 50  -> \"plan a /compact\"\n  CTX_COMPACT_PCT  default 60  -> \"/compact at the next green test\"\n  urgent = CTX_COMPACT_PCT + 20 -> \"compact or /handoff now; consider splitting\"\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nNONE, WARN, COMPACT, URGENT = 0, 1, 2, 3\n\n\ndef thresholds() -> tuple[float, float, float]:\n    warn = float(os.environ.get(\"CTX_WARN_PCT\", 50))\n    compact = float(os.environ.get(\"CTX_COMPACT_PCT\", 60))\n    return warn, compact, compact + 20\n\n\ndef band_for(pct: float, warn: float, compact: float, urgent: float) -> int:\n    if pct >= urgent:\n        return URGENT\n    if pct >= compact:\n        return COMPACT\n    if pct >= warn:\n        return WARN\n    return NONE\n\n\ndef message_for(band: int, pct: float) -> str:\n    p = f\"{pct:.0f}%\"\n    return {\n        WARN: (\n            f\"Context {p}: finish the current red-green cycle, then plan a \"\n            \"/compact at the next green test.\"\n        ),\n        COMPACT: (\n            f\"Context {p}: at the next green test run  /compact keep: ticket #N \"\n            \"acceptance criteria, what is left, files touched\"\n        ),\n        URGENT: (\n            f\"Context {p}: compact now (or /handoff and start fresh). If this \"\n            \"ticket keeps overflowing, split it with /to-tickets.\"\n        ),\n    }[band]\n\n\ndef decide(pct: float, last_band: int) -> tuple[str | None, int]:\n    \"\"\"Pure function (unit-tested): return (message or None, band to store).\n\n    Warn once per upward crossing. When usage drops (after /compact or\n    /clear), store the lower band so the next crossing warns again.\n    \"\"\"\n    raise NotImplementedError(\"MP2 Exercise A: implement me with /tdd\")\n\nLEDGER_COLUMNS = [\n    \"ts\", \"session\", \"phase\", \"provider\", \"model\", \"effort\",\n    \"ctx_pct\", \"five_h_pct\", \"weekly_pct\",\n]\n\n\ndef log_usage(project: Path, session: str, state: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Append one row per turn to notes/usage-ledger.csv (rubric item B7).\"\"\"\n    ledger = project / \"notes\" / \"usage-ledger.csv\"\n    phase_file = project / \".claude\" / \"state\" / \"phase\"\n    try:\n        phase = phase_file.read_text().strip() or \"unlabelled\"\n    except OSError:\n        phase = \"unlabelled\"\n\n    def cell(value: object) -> str:\n        return \"\" if value is None else str(value)\n\n    row = [\n        datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\"),\n        session[:8], phase, cell(state.get(\"provider\")), cell(state.get(\"model\")),\n        cell(state.get(\"effort\")), f\"{float(state.get('pct', 0)):.1f}\",\n        cell(state.get(\"five_h\")), cell(state.get(\"weekly\")),\n    ]\n    try:\n        ledger.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        new = not ledger.exists()\n        with ledger.open(\"a\", newline=\"\") as f:\n            writer = csv.writer(f)\n            if new:\n                writer.writerow(LEDGER_COLUMNS)\n            writer.writerow(row)\n    except OSError:\n        pass\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    try:\n        event = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except Exception:\n        return\n    project = Path(os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\") or event.get(\"cwd\") or os.getcwd())\n    session = event.get(\"session_id\") or \"default\"\n    state_dir = project / \".claude\" / \"state\"\n    ctx_file = state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}.json\"\n    band_file = state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}_band.json\"\n\n    try:\n        state = json.loads(ctx_file.read_text())\n        pct = float(state[\"pct\"])\n    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):\n        return  # no status-line data yet: stay silent\n    try:\n        last_band = int(json.loads(band_file.read_text())[\"band\"])\n    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):\n        last_band = NONE\n\n    log_usage(project, session, state)\n\n    msg, band = decide(pct, last_band)\n    try:\n        band_file.write_text(json.dumps({\"band\": band}))\n    except OSError:\n        pass\n    if msg:\n \"trigger\": \"manual\",\n         \"custom_instructions\": \"keep: ticket #5 criteria\"}, tmp_path)\n    log = (tmp_path / \"docs/process/compaction-log.md\").read_text()\n    assert \"| manual | keep: ticket #5 criteria |\" in log\n{\n  \"statusLine\": {\n    \"type\": \"command\",\n    \"command\": \"python3 \\\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}/.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\\\"\"\n  },\n  \"hooks\": {\n    \"Stop\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 10 } ] }\n    ],\n    \"PreCompact\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/ctx_compact_log.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 10 } ] }\n    ],\n    \"SessionEnd\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/save_transcript.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 15 } ] }\n    ]\n  }\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"563155ae-209c-4020-a0fc-c9748847853e","timestamp":"2026-10-13T00:13:11.298Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Stop hook: warn when the context window crosses 50% / 60% / 80%.\n\nClaude Code runs this after every Claude turn (the Stop event) and pipes the\nevent JSON to stdin. It reads the percentage the status line recorded, and\nprints {\"systemMessage\": \"...\"} the first time usage crosses a threshold.\nThe message is shown to YOU, not to Claude. The hook never blocks and never\nruns /compact itself: slash commands are yours to type.\n\nThresholds (override when launching:  CTX_WARN_PCT=1 claude  to test):\n  CTX_WARN_PCT     default 50  -> \"plan a /compact\"\n  CTX_COMPACT_PCT  default 60  -> \"/compact at the next green test\"\n  urgent = CTX_COMPACT_PCT + 20 -> \"compact or /handoff now; consider splitting\"\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nNONE, WARN, COMPACT, URGENT = 0, 1, 2, 3\n\n\ndef thresholds() -> tuple[float, float, float]:\n    warn = float(os.environ.get(\"CTX_WARN_PCT\", 50))\n    compact = float(os.environ.get(\"CTX_COMPACT_PCT\", 60))\n    return warn, compact, compact + 20\n\n\ndef band_for(pct: float, warn: float, compact: float, urgent: float) -> int:\n    if pct >= urgent:\n        return URGENT\n    if pct >= compact:\n        return COMPACT\n    if pct >= warn:\n        return WARN\n    return NONE\n\n\ndef message_for(band: int, pct: float) -> str:\n    p = f\"{pct:.0f}%\"\n    return {\n        WARN: (\n            f\"Context {p}: finish the current red-green cycle, then plan a \"\n            \"/compact at the next green test.\"\n        ),\n        COMPACT: (\n            f\"Context {p}: at the next green test run  /compact keep: ticket #N \"\n            \"acceptance criteria, what is left, files touched\"\n        ),\n        URGENT: (\n            f\"Context {p}: compact now (or /handoff and start fresh). If this \"\n            \"ticket keeps overflowing, split it with /to-tickets.\"\n        ),\n    }[band]\n\n\ndef decide(pct: float, last_band: int) -> tuple[str | None, int]:\n    \"\"\"Pure function (unit-tested): return (message or None, band to store).\n\n    Warn once per upward crossing. When usage drops (after /compact or\n    /clear), store the lower band so the next crossing warns again.\n    \"\"\"\n    raise NotImplementedError(\"MP2 Exercise A: implement me with /tdd\")\n\nLEDGER_COLUMNS = [\n    \"ts\", \"session\", \"phase\", \"provider\", \"model\", \"effort\",\n    \"ctx_pct\", \"five_h_pct\", \"weekly_pct\",\n]\n\n\ndef log_usage(project: Path, session: str, state: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Append one row per turn to notes/usage-ledger.csv (rubric item B7).\"\"\"\n    ledger = project / \"notes\" / \"usage-ledger.csv\"\n    phase_file = project / \".claude\" / \"state\" / \"phase\"\n    try:\n        phase = phase_file.read_text().strip() or \"unlabelled\"\n    except OSError:\n        phase = \"unlabelled\"\n\n    def cell(value: object) -> str:\n        return \"\" if value is None else str(value)\n\n    row = [\n        datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\"),\n        session[:8], phase, cell(state.get(\"provider\")), cell(state.get(\"model\")),\n        cell(state.get(\"effort\")), f\"{float(state.get('pct', 0)):.1f}\",\n        cell(state.get(\"five_h\")), cell(state.get(\"weekly\")),\n    ]\n    try:\n        ledger.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        new = not ledger.exists()\n        with ledger.open(\"a\", newline=\"\") as f:\n            writer = csv.writer(f)\n            if new:\n                writer.writerow(LEDGER_COLUMNS)\n            writer.writerow(row)\n    except OSError:\n        pass\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    try:\n        event = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except Exception:\n        return\n    project = Path(os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\") or event.get(\"cwd\") or os.getcwd())\n    session = event.get(\"session_id\") or \"default\"\n    state_dir = project / \".claude\" / \"state\"\n    ctx_file = state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}.json\"\n    band_file = state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}_band.json\"\n\n    try:\n        state = json.loads(ctx_file.read_text())\n        pct = float(state[\"pct\"])\n    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):\n        return  # no status-line data yet: stay silent\n    try:\n        last_band = int(json.loads(band_file.read_text())[\"band\"])\n    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):\n        last_band = NONE\n\n    log_usage(project, session, state)\n\n    msg, band = decide(pct, last_band)\n    try:\n        band_file.write_text(json.dumps({\"band\": band}))\n    except OSError:\n        pass\n    if msg:\n        print(json.dumps({\"systemMessage\": msg}))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    try:\n        main()\n    except Exception:\n        pass  # a warning hook must never break the session\n    sys.exit(0)\n\"\"\"Tests for the context-threshold hooks (IPHS 400 MP2, setup exercise).\"\"\"\nimport importlib.util\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nHOOKS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / \".claude\" / \"hooks\"\n\n\ndef load(name):\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, HOOKS / f\"{name}.py\")\n    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)\n    return mod\n\n\nguard = load(\"ctx_guard\")\n\n\ndef run(script, payload, project, **env):\n    return subprocess.run(\n        [sys.executable, str(HOOKS / script)],\n        input=json.dumps(payload),\n        capture_output=True,\n        text=True,\n        env={**os.environ, \"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\": str(project), **env},\n    )\n\n\n# ---- pure logic -----------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_silent_below_50():\n    assert guard.decide(49.9, guard.NONE) == (None, guard.NONE)\n\n\ndef test_warns_once_at_50():\n    msg, band = guard.decide(52, guard.NONE)\n    assert band == guard.WARN and \"52%\" in msg\n    assert guard.decide(55, band) == (None, guard.WARN)  # no nagging\n\n\ndef test_compact_message_at_60_names_focus_note():\n    msg, band = guard.decide(61, guard.WARN)\n    assert band == guard.COMPACT and \"/compact keep:\" in msg\n\n\ndef test_urgent_at_80_suggests_split():\n    msg, band = guard.decide(81, guard.COMPACT)\n    assert band == guard.URGENT and \"/to-tickets\" in msg\n\n\ndef test_rearms_after_compaction():\n    _, band = guard.decide(20, guard.COMPACT)   # usage dropped after /compact\n    assert band == guard.NONE\n    msg, _ = guard.decide(51, band)             # next crossing warns again\n    assert msg is not None\n\n\ndef test_thresholds_from_env(monkeypatch):\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CTX_WARN_PCT\", \"1\")\n    msg, _ = guard.decide(2, guard.NONE)\n    assert msg is not None\n\n\n# ---- end to end: status line -> Stop hook ---------------------------------\n\ndef test_statusline_then_stop_hook(tmp_path):\n    sl = run(\"ctx_statusline.py\",\n             {\"session_id\": \"s1\", \"model\": {\"display_name\": \"Opus\"},\n              \"context_window\": {\"used_percentage\": 63.4}}, tmp_path)\n    assert \"ctx 63%\" in sl.stdout\n    out = run(\"ctx_guard.py\", {\"session_id\": \"s1\", \"hook_event_name\": \"Stop\"}, tmp_path)\n    assert out.returncode == 0\n    assert \"/compact\" in json.loads(out.stdout)[\"systemMessage\"]\n    again = run(\"ctx_guard.py\", {\"session_id\": \"s1\", \"hook_event_name\": \"Stop\"}, tmp_path)\n    assert again.stdout.strip() == \"\"           # warned once, then quiet\n\n\ndef test_stop_hook_silent_without_data(tmp_path):\n    out = run(\"ctx_guard.py\", {\"session_id\": \"nobody\"}, tmp_path)\n    assert out.returncode == 0 and out.stdout == \"\"\n\n\ndef test_stop_hook_survives_garbage_stdin(tmp_path):\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(HOOKS / \"ctx_guard.py\")],\n                         input=\"not json\", capture_output=True, text=True,\n                         env={**os.environ, \"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\": str(tmp_path)})\n    assert out.returncode == 0\n\n\ndef test_precompact_logs_focus_note(tmp_path):\n    run(\"ctx_compact_log.py\",\n        {\"session_id\": \"abcdef123\", \"trigger\": \"manual\",\n         \"custom_instructions\": \"keep: ticket #5 criteria\"}, tmp_path)\n    log = (tmp_path / \"docs/process/compaction-log.md\").read_text()\n    assert \"| manual | keep: ticket #5 criteria |\" in log\n{\n  \"statusLine\": {\n    \"type\": \"command\",\n    \"command\": \"python3 \\\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}/.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\\\"\"\n  },\n  \"hooks\": {\n    \"Stop\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 10 } ] }\n    ],\n    \"PreCompact\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/ctx_compact_log.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 10 } ] }\n    ],\n    \"SessionEnd\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/save_transcript.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 15 } ] }\n    ]\n  }\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"5d5e50d0-3e20-438b-a5df-6fc50e39c079","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":42},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"89050fd4-e9d6-44fb-ad4f-f89e54b9f1a2","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"65c6e9e5-c88f-4ccb-b0ed-80a13b48a9de","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"2a5fa244-5ee5-45be-a093-94e0e37d9a8d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SPG8vQnAgY36tgbMc3of4E","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"d42fda60-b76d-40a1-94bf-f80f21fad226","timestamp":"2026-10-13T00:13:17.539Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py","oldString":"    raise NotImplementedError(\"MP2 Exercise A: implement me with /tdd\")","newString":"    band = band_for(pct, *thresholds())\n    if band > last_band:\n        return message_for(band, pct), band\n    return None, band","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Stop hook: warn when the context window crosses 50% / 60% / 80%.\n\nClaude Code runs this after every Claude turn (the Stop event) and pipes the\nevent JSON to stdin. It reads the percentage the status line recorded, and\nprints {\"systemMessage\": \"...\"} the first time usage crosses a threshold.\nThe message is shown to YOU, not to Claude. The hook never blocks and never\nruns /compact itself: slash commands are yours to type.\n\nThresholds (override when launching:  CTX_WARN_PCT=1 claude  to test):\n  CTX_WARN_PCT     default 50  -> \"plan a /compact\"\n  CTX_COMPACT_PCT  default 60  -> \"/compact at the next green test\"\n  urgent = CTX_COMPACT_PCT + 20 -> \"compact or /handoff now; consider splitting\"\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nNONE, WARN, COMPACT, URGENT = 0, 1, 2, 3\n\n\ndef thresholds() -> tuple[float, float, float]:\n    warn = float(os.environ.get(\"CTX_WARN_PCT\", 50))\n    compact = float(os.environ.get(\"CTX_COMPACT_PCT\", 60))\n    return warn, compact, compact + 20\n\n\ndef band_for(pct: float, warn: float, compact: float, urgent: float) -> int:\n    if pct >= urgent:\n        return URGENT\n    if pct >= compact:\n        return COMPACT\n    if pct >= warn:\n        return WARN\n    return NONE\n\n\ndef message_for(band: int, pct: float) -> str:\n    p = f\"{pct:.0f}%\"\n    return {\n        WARN: (\n            f\"Context {p}: finish the current red-green cycle, then plan a \"\n            \"/compact at the next green test.\"\n        ),\n        COMPACT: (\n            f\"Context {p}: at the next green test run  /compact keep: ticket #N \"\n            \"acceptance criteria, what is left, files touched\"\n        ),\n        URGENT: (\n            f\"Context {p}: compact now (or /handoff and start fresh). If this \"\n            \"ticket keeps overflowing, split it with /to-tickets.\"\n        ),\n    }[band]\n\n\ndef decide(pct: float, last_band: int) -> tuple[str | None, int]:\n    \"\"\"Pure function (unit-tested): return (message or None, band to store).\n\n    Warn once per upward crossing. When usage drops (after /compact or\n    /clear), store the lower band so the next crossing warns again.\n    \"\"\"\n    raise NotImplementedError(\"MP2 Exercise A: implement me with /tdd\")\n\nLEDGER_COLUMNS = [\n    \"ts\", \"session\", \"phase\", \"provider\", \"model\", \"effort\",\n    \"ctx_pct\", \"five_h_pct\", \"weekly_pct\",\n]\n\n\ndef log_usage(project: Path, session: str, state: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Append one row per turn to notes/usage-ledger.csv (rubric item B7).\"\"\"\n    ledger = project / \"notes\" / \"usage-ledger.csv\"\n    phase_file = project / \".claude\" / \"state\" / \"phase\"\n    try:\n        phase = phase_file.read_text().strip() or \"unlabelled\"\n    except OSError:\n        phase = \"unlabelled\"\n\n    def cell(value: object) -> str:\n        return \"\" if value is None else str(value)\n\n    row = [\n        datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\"),\n        session[:8], phase, cell(state.get(\"provider\")), cell(state.get(\"model\")),\n        cell(state.get(\"effort\")), f\"{float(state.get('pct', 0)):.1f}\",\n        cell(state.get(\"five_h\")), cell(state.get(\"weekly\")),\n    ]\n    try:\n        ledger.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        new = not ledger.exists()\n        with ledger.open(\"a\", newline=\"\") as f:\n            writer = csv.writer(f)\n            if new:\n                writer.writerow(LEDGER_COLUMNS)\n            writer.writerow(row)\n    except OSError:\n        pass\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    try:\n        event = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except Exception:\n        return\n    project = Path(os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\") or event.get(\"cwd\") or os.getcwd())\n    session = event.get(\"session_id\") or \"default\"\n    state_dir = project / \".claude\" / \"state\"\n    ctx_file = state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}.json\"\n    band_file = state_dir / f\"ctx_{session}_band.json\"\n\n    try:\n        state = json.loads(ctx_file.read_text())\n        pct = float(state[\"pct\"])\n    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):\n        return  # no status-line data yet: stay silent\n    try:\n        last_band = int(json.loads(band_file.read_text())[\"band\"])\n    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):\n        last_band = NONE\n\n    log_usage(project, session, state)\n\n    msg, band = decide(pct, last_band)\n    try:\n        band_file.write_text(json.dumps({\"band\": band}))\n    except OSError:\n        pass\n    if msg:\n        print(json.dumps({\"systemMessage\": msg}))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    try:\n        main()\n    except Exception:\n        pass  # a warning hook must never break the session\n    sys.exit(0)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":64,"oldLines":7,"newStart":64,"newLines":10,"lines":["     Warn once per upward crossing. {"parentUuid":"9d5baf20-ab09-4187-af38-5f0b12d62c39","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"73ca2637-e8be-4805-9d58-76c6f0ecb291","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01J8dwiTCng5uTnppkD9ZR4y","type":"tool_result","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Turn notes/usage-ledger.csv into a plan-vs-actual budget report.\n\nUsage:\n    uv run python scripts/usage_report.py                 # summary + forecast\n    uv run python scripts/usage_report.py --remaining 4   # 4 tickets still to do\n\nWhy positive deltas: the 5-hour and weekly meters RESET (drop to 0) when their\nwindow rolls over, and the weekly one only ever climbs inside a week. Summing\nonly the increases between consecutive rows measures what you actually spent\nand ignores the resets. That is what spend() does, and it is your exercise.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport csv\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nLEDGER = Path(\"notes/usage-ledger.csv\")\n\n\ndef spend(series: list[float | None]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Total rise across a meter that can reset to a lower value.\n\n    Sum every increase between consecutive readings; ignore blanks (None) and\n    any drop, which means the window reset.\n\n    >>> spend([10.0, 12.0, 15.0])\n    5.0\n    >>> spend([80.0, 5.0, 9.0])\n    4.0\n    >>> spend([None, 4.0, None, 7.0])\n    3.0\n    \"\"\"\n    raise NotImplementedError(\"MP2 Exercise B: implement me with /tdd\")\n\ndef read_rows(path: Path) -> list[dict]:\n    with path.open(newline=\"\") as f:\n        return list(csv.DictReader(f))\n\n\ndef to_float(text: str | None) -> float | None:\n    try:\n        return float(text) if text not in (None, \"\") else None\n    except ValueError:\n        return None\n\n\ndef by_phase(rows: list[dict]) -> dict[str, dict]:\n    phases: dict[str, dict] = defaultdict(\n        lambda: {\"turns\": 0, \"five\": [], \"weekly\": [], \"models\": set(), \"providers\": set()}\n    )\n    for row in rows:\n        phase = phases[row.get(\"phase\") or \"unlabelled\"]\n        phase[\"turns\"] += 1\n        phase[\"five\"].append(to_float(row.get(\"five_h_pct\")))\n        phase[\"weekly\"].append(to_float(row.get(\"weekly_pct\")))\n        if row.get(\"model\"):\n            phase[\"models\"].add(row[\"model\"])\n        if row.get(\"provider\"):\n            phase[\"providers\"].add(row[\"provider\"])\n    return phases\n\n\ndef ticket_phases(phases: dict[str, dict]) -> list[str]:\n    return sorted(p for p in phases if p.upper().startswith(\"T\") and p[1:].isdigit())\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--ledger\", type=Path, default=LEDGER)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--remaining\", type=int, default=0, help=\"tickets not yet built\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    if not args.ledger.exists():\n        raise SystemExit(f\"No ledger at {args.ledger}. Is the Stop hook registered?\")\n    rows = read_rows(args.ledger)\n    if not rows:\n        raise SystemExit(\"Ledger is empty.\")\n    phases = by_phase(rows)\n\n    print(f\"{'phase':<14}{'turns':>6}{'5h spent':>10}{'weekly':>9}  models / provider\")\n    for name, data in sorted(phases.items()):\n        mix = \",\".join(sorted(data[\"models\"])) or \"?\"\n        prov = \",\".join(sorted(data[\"providers\"] - {\"anthropic\"}))\n        print(f\"{name:<14}{data['turns']:>6}{spend(data['five']):>9.1f}%\"\n              f\"{spend(data['weekly']):>8.1f}%  {mix}{(' @' + prov) if prov else ''}\")\n\n    tickets = ticket_phases(phases)\n    if tickets:\n        per = sum(spend(phases[t][\"weekly\"]) for t in tickets) / len(tickets)\n        print(f\"\\nTickets done: {len(tickets)} · average weekly cost per ticket: {per:.1f}%\")\n        last = next((to_float(r.get(\"weekly_pct\")) for r in reversed(rows)\n                     if to_float(r.get(\"weekly_pct\")) is not None), None)\n        if last is not None and args.remaining:\n            need, headroom = per * args.remaining, 100 - last\n            verdict = \"FITS\" if need <= headroom * 0.8 else \"TIGHT\" if need <= headroom else \"OVER BUDGET\"\n            print(f\"Forecast: {args.remaining} tickets need ~{need:.0f}% of the weekly cap; \"\n                  f\"{headroom:.0f}% remains → {verdict}\")\n            if verdict != \"FITS\":\n                print(\"Fix in this order: 1) lower effort  2) switch to Sonnet/Haiku  \"\n                      \"3) /clear more often  4) split fat tickets  5) Plan B backend (declare it)\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\nNo ticket phases yet. Write the ticket id into .claude/state/phase \"\n              \"at the start of each /implement session.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n\"\"\"Tests for the token-budget report (IPHS 400 MP2, exercise 2).\"\"\"\nimport importlib.util\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nspec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(\"usage_report\", ROOT / \"scripts\" / \"usage_report.py\")\nur = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\nspec.loader.exec_module(ur)\n\nHEADER = \"ts,session,phase,provider,model,effort,ctx_pct,five_h_pct,weekly_pct\\n\"\n\n\ndef ledger(tmp_path, *rows):\n    p = tmp_path / \"usage-ledger.csv\"\n    p.write_text(HEADER + \"\".join(rows))\n    return p\n\n\ndef row(phase, five, weekly, model=\"Sonnet\", provider=\"anthropic\"):\n    return f\"2026-09-30T10:00:00Z,abc,{phase},{provider},{model},medium,40.0,{five},{weekly}\\n\"\n\n\ndef test_spend_counts_only_increases():\n    assert ur.spend([10.0, 12.0, 15.0]) == 5.0\n\n\ndef test_spend_ignores_window_reset():\n    assert ur.spend([80.0, 5.0, 9.0]) == 4.0\n\n\ndef test_spend_skips_blanks():\n    assert ur.spend([None, 4.0, None, 7.0]) == 3.0\n\n\ndef test_spend_empty_is_zero():\n    assert ur.spend([]) == 0.0 and ur.spend([None, None]) == 0.0\n\n\ndef test_report_groups_by_phase(tmp_path):\n    p = ledger(tmp_path, row(\"grill\", 5, 2, model=\"Opus\"), row(\"grill\", 15, 6, model=\"Opus\"),\n               row(\"T01\", 20, 8), row(\"T01\", 30, 12))\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts/usage_report.py\"),\n                          \"--ledger\", str(p)], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    assert \"grill\" in out.stdout and \"10.0%\" in out.stdout   # 5 -> 15\n    assert \"Opus\" in out.stdout\n    assert \"average weekly cost per ticket: 4.0%\" in out.stdout\n\n\ndef test_forecast_flags_over_budget(tmp_path):\n    p = ledger(tmp_path, row(\"T01\", 10, 60), row(\"T01\", 40, 80))\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts/usage_report.py\"),\n                          \"--ledger\", str(p), \"--remaining\", \"4\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    assert \"OVER BUDGET\" in out.stdout and \"split fat tickets\" in out.stdout\n\n\ndef test_missing_ledger_explains(tmp_path):\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts/usage_report.py\"),\n                          \"--ledger\", str(tmp_path / \"nope.csv\")], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    assert out.returncode != 0 and \"Stop hook\" in out.stderr\n\n\ndef test_backup_provider_shown(tmp_path):\n    p = ledger(tmp_path, row(\"T05\", 0, 10, model=\"glm-5.3\", provider=\"api.z.ai\"),\n               row(\"T05\", 5, 14, model=\"glm-5.3\", provider=\"api.z.ai\"))\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts/usage_report.py\"),\n                          \"--ledger\", str(p)], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    assert \"@api.z.ai\" in out.stdout\n# Token budget plan\n\n(Part 5.4 of the manual. Fill this in before your first /implement.)\nts,session,phase,provider,model,effort,ctx_pct,five_h_pct,weekly_pct\r\n2026-09-29T19:24:29Z,86c96d58,unlabelled,anthropic,Sonnet 5.5,medium,4.0,1,3\r\n2026-09-29T19:25:16Z,86c96d58,unlabelled,anthropic,Sonnet 5.5,medium,4.0,1,3\r\n2026-09-29T19:25:51Z,86c96d58,unlabelled,anthropic,Sonnet 5.5,medium,4.0,1,3\r\n2026-09-29T19:27:04Z,86c96d58,unlabelled,anthropic,Sonnet 5.5,medium,5.0,1,3","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"b9bc15f6-1d2e-41ba-8024-9985f10f70f3","timestamp":"2026-10-13T00:13:46.859Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Turn notes/usage-ledger.csv into a plan-vs-actual budget report.\n\nUsage:\n    uv run python scripts/usage_report.py                 # summary + forecast\n    uv run python scripts/usage_report.py --remaining 4   # 4 tickets still to do\n\nWhy positive deltas: the 5-hour and weekly meters RESET (drop to 0) when their\nwindow rolls over, and the weekly one only ever climbs inside a week. Write the ticket id into .claude/state/phase \"\n              \"at the start of each /implement session.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n\"\"\"Tests for the token-budget report (IPHS 400 MP2, exercise 2).\"\"\"\nimport importlib.util\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nspec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(\"usage_report\", ROOT / \"scripts\" / \"usage_report.py\")\nur = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\nspec.loader.exec_module(ur)\n\nHEADER = \"ts,session,phase,provider,model,effort,ctx_pct,five_h_pct,weekly_pct\\n\"\n\n\ndef ledger(tmp_path, *rows):\n    p = tmp_path / \"usage-ledger.csv\"\n    p.write_text(HEADER + \"\".join(rows))\n    return p\n\n\ndef row(phase, five, weekly, model=\"Sonnet\", provider=\"anthropic\"):\n    return f\"2026-09-30T10:00:00Z,abc,{phase},{provider},{model},medium,40.0,{five},{weekly}\\n\"\n\n\ndef test_spend_counts_only_increases():\n    assert ur.spend([10.0, 12.0, 15.0]) == 5.0\n\n\ndef test_spend_ignores_window_reset():\n    assert ur.spend([80.0, 5.0, 9.0]) == 4.0\n\n\ndef test_spend_skips_blanks():\n    assert ur.spend([None, 4.0, None, 7.0]) == 3.0\n\n\ndef test_spend_empty_is_zero():\n    assert ur.spend([]) == 0.0 and ur.spend([None, None]) == 0.0\n\n\ndef test_report_groups_by_phase(tmp_path):\n    p = ledger(tmp_path, row(\"grill\", 5, 2, model=\"Opus\"), row(\"grill\", 15, 6, model=\"Opus\"),\n               row(\"T01\", 20, 8), row(\"T01\", 30, 12))\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts/usage_report.py\"),\n                          \"--ledger\", str(p)], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    assert \"grill\" in out.stdout and \"10.0%\" in out.stdout   # 5 -> 15\n    assert \"Opus\" in out.stdout\n    assert \"average weekly cost per ticket: 4.0%\" in out.stdout\n\n\ndef test_forecast_flags_over_budget(tmp_path):\n    p = ledger(tmp_path, row(\"T01\", 10, 60), row(\"T01\", 40, 80))\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts/usage_report.py\"),\n                          \"--ledger\", str(p), \"--remaining\", \"4\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    assert \"OVER BUDGET\" in out.stdout and \"split fat tickets\" in out.stdout\n\n\ndef test_missing_ledger_explains(tmp_path):\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts/usage_report.py\"),\n                          \"--ledger\", str(tmp_path / \"nope.csv\")], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    assert out.returncode != 0 and \"Stop hook\" in out.stderr\n\n\ndef test_backup_provider_shown(tmp_path):\n    p = ledger(tmp_path, row(\"T05\", 0, 10, model=\"glm-5.3\", provider=\"api.z.ai\"),\n               row(\"T05\", 5, 14, model=\"glm-5.3\", provider=\"api.z.ai\"))\n    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts/usage_report.py\"),\n                          \"--ledger\", str(p)], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    assert \"@api.z.ai\" in out.stdout\n# Token budget plan\n\n(Part 5.4 of the manual. Fill this in before your first /implement.)\nts,session,phase,provider,model,effort,ctx_pct,five_h_pct,weekly_pct\r\n2026-09-29T19:24:29Z,86c96d58,unlabelled,anthropic,Sonnet 5.5,medium,4.0,1,3\r\n2026-09-29T19:25:16Z,86c96d58,unlabelled,anthropic,Sonnet 5.5,medium,4.0,1,3\r\n2026-09-29T19:25:51Z,86c96d58,unlabelled,anthropic,Sonnet 5.5,medium,4.0,1,3\r\n2026-09-29T19:27:04Z,86c96d58,unlabelled,anthropic,Sonnet 5.5,medium,5.0,1,3","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"85e6b30e-2263-452b-9d21-9032ae26da44","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":2,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":42},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9d5baf20-ab09-4187-af38-5f0b12d62c39","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"fe357cf3-6237-436c-a68e-65168868e770","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"73ca2637-e8be-4805-9d58-76c6f0ecb291","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Jb2NX4DPVd4X9iw4qUuBez","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/scripts/usage_report.py has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"6b8b975b-27d1-4e70-a5a7-5dca84ef69f6","timestamp":"2026-10-13T00:13:50.762Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/scripts/usage_report.py","oldString":"    raise NotImplementedError(\"MP2 Exercise B: implement me with /tdd\")","newString":"    total = 0.0\n    previous = None\n    for value in series:\n        if value is None:\n            continue\n        if previous is not None and value > previous:\n            total += value - previous\n        previous = value\n    return total","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Turn notes/usage-ledger.csv into a plan-vs-actual budget report.\n\nUsage:\n    uv run python scripts/usage_report.py                 # summary + forecast\n    uv run python scripts/usage_report.py --remaining 4   # 4 tickets still to do\n\nWhy positive deltas: the 5-hour and weekly meters RESET (drop to 0) when their\nwindow rolls over, and the weekly one only ever climbs inside a week. Summing\nonly the increases between consecutive rows measures what you actually spent\nand ignores the resets. That is what spend() does, and it is your exercise.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport csv\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nLEDGER = Path(\"notes/usage-ledger.csv\")\n\n\ndef spend(series: list[float | None]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Total rise across a meter that can reset to a lower value.\n\n    Sum every increase between consecutive readings; ignore blanks (None) and\n    any drop, which means the window reset.\n\n    >>> spend([10.0, 12.0, 15.0])\n    5.0\n    >>> spend([80.0, 5.0, 9.0])\n    4.0\n    >>> spend([None, 4.0, None, 7.0])\n    3.0\n    \"\"\"\n    raise NotImplementedError(\"MP2 Exercise B: implement me with /tdd\")\n\ndef read_rows(path: Path) -> list[dict]:\n    with path.open(newline=\"\") as f:\n        return list(csv.DictReader(f))\n\n\ndef to_float(text: str | None) -> float | None:\n    try:\n        return float(text) if text not in (None, \"\") else None\n    except ValueError:\n        return None\n\n\ndef by_phase(rows: list[dict]) -> dict[str, dict]:\n    phases: dict[str, dict] = defaultdict(\n        lambda: {\"turns\": 0, \"five\": [], \"weekly\": [], \"models\": set(), \"providers\": set()}\n    )\n    for row in rows:\n        phase = phases[row.get(\"phase\") or \"unlabelled\"]\n        phase[\"turns\"] += 1\n        phase[\"five\"].append(to_float(row.get(\"five_h_pct\")))\n        phase[\"weekly\"].append(to_float(row.get(\"weekly_pct\")))\n        if row.get(\"model\"):\n            phase[\"models\"].add(row[\"model\"])\n        if row.get(\"provider\"):\n            phase[\"providers\"].add(row[\"provider\"])\n    return phases\n\n\ndef ticket_phases(phases: dict[str, dict]) -> list[str]:\n    return sorted(p for p in phases if p.upper().startswith(\"T\") and p[1:].isdigit())\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--ledger\", type=Path, default=LEDGER)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--remaining\", type=int, default=0, help=\"tickets not yet built\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    if not args.ledger.exists():\n        raise SystemExit(f\"No ledger at {args.ledger}. Is the Stop hook registered?\")\n    rows = read_rows(args.ledger)\n    if not rows:\n        raise SystemExit(\"Ledger is empty.\")\n    phases = by_phase(rows)\n\n    print(f\"{'phase':<14}{'turns':>6}{'5h spent':>10}{'weekly':>9}  models / provider\")\n    for name, data in sorted(phases.items()):\n        mix = \",\".join(sorted(data[\"models\"])) or \"?\"\n        prov = \",\".join(sorted(data[\"providers\"] - {\"anthropic\"}))\n        print(f\"{name:<14}{data['turns']:>6}{spend(data['five']):>9.1f}%\"\n              f\"{spend(data['weekly']):>8.1f}%  {mix}{(' @' + prov) if prov else ''}\")\n\n    tickets = ticket_phases(phases)\n    if tickets:\n        per = sum(spend(phases[t][\"weekly\"]) for t in tickets) / len(tickets)\n        print(f\"\\nTickets done: {len(tickets)} · average weekly cost per ticket: {per:.1f}%\")\n        last = next((to_float(r.get(\"weekly_pct\")) for r in reversed(rows)\n                     if to_float(r.get(\"weekly_pct\")) is not None), None)\n        if last is not None and args.remaining:\n            need, headroom = per * args.remaining, 100 - last\n            verdict = \"FITS\" if need <= headroom * 0.8 else \"TIGHT\" if need <= headroom else \"OVER BUDGET\"\n            print(f\"Forecast: {args.remaining} tickets need ~{need:.0f}% of the weekly cap; \"\n                  f\"{headroom:.0f}% remains → {verdict}\")\n            if verdict != \"FITS\":\n                print(\"Fix in this order: 1) lower effort  2) switch to Sonnet/Haiku  \"\n                      \"3) /clear more often  4) split fat tickets  5) Plan B backend (declare it)\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\nNo ticket phases yet. Write the ticket id into .claude/state/phase \"\n              \"at the start of each /implement session.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":33,"oldLines":7,"newStart":33,"newLines":15,"lines":["     >>> spend([None, 4.0, None, 7.0])","     3.0","     \"\"\"","-    raise NotImplementedError(\"MP2 Exercise B: implement me with /tdd\")","+    total = 0.0","+    previous = None","+    for value in series:","+        if value is None:","+            continue","+        if previous is not None and value > previous:","+            total += value - previous","+        previous = value","+    return total"," "," def read_rows(path: Path) -> list[dict]:","     with path.open(newline=\"\") as f:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"contentNotInModelContext":true},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fe357cf3-6237-436c-a68e-65168868e770","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"c17eaf71-0e18-4dd5-a81f-13afce019616","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfyGsE8kpS68BYrQBQQ4R","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Agreed. We're on Part 2 now, and I'll follow the manual in order from here.\n\n**2.1 Field trip: WordPress Playground**\n\nOpen `https://playground.wordpress.net` in Chrome. Nothing installs, and closing the tab wipes it. Do these six in order:\n\n1. Write a **post** with a title and a paragraph, and click **Save draft**. Open the public site in another tab. It shouldn't be there.\n2. **Publish** the post, then reload the public site. Now it should appear.\n3. Write a **page** called \"About\". Notice where pages show up that posts don't. It's the navigation menu.\n4. Open **Settings → Permalinks**. See how the URL is built from the title. That string is the slug.\n5. Go to **Users → Add New** and create a user with the **Editor** role. Log out and log in as that user. Try to reach **Users**, and write down exactly what happens.\n6. Find where **Posts → All Posts** lets you filter by status. That's the content list you'll build.\n\n**2.2 Field notes**\n\nWhen you're done, send me your observations in your own words. You need at least 5 and can write more. The manual suggests noting:\n- what surprised you\n- what was hidden\n- what took more clicks than you expected\n- what you couldn't find\n- what an editor couldn't do\n\nWrite them like you're telling a friend. I'll put them into `notes/cms-field-notes.md` with only light cleanup, such as formatting. I won't add observations you didn't make, because this file is graded under S1.1 and A2 and has to be your own.\n\n**2.3 Vocabulary**\n\nAfter the notes, the manual says to run `/clear` and then:\n\n```text\n/research how content management systems model content, users and roles, and publishing — compare WordPress, Drupal, and static-site generators\n```\n\nThat's your command to type, since `/research` is meant to run in your own session. It's low token cost.\n\nGo ahead with the Playground steps. Save the draft first if it asks.\n3. In the left sidebar, open **Settings**, then **Permalinks**.\n\n**The \"About\" item is a Post**\nThe header says \"About · Post\", so you've created a post called About. Step 3 wants a **Page**. To make one:\n- In the left sidebar, go to **Pages → Add New Page**.\n- Title it \"About\" and publish it.\n- Then compare where it appears on the public site (the navigation) against your post.\n\nYour Step 3 note could include that you started it as a post by mistake, if that's honest. It's the kind of specific detail the rubric values.\n\n**The slug, as a fallback**\nThe right panel is on the **Block** tab.
